=== OBSERVATIONS FOR AUDITOR / NEXT PASS ===
  - What ReliefWeb says about the window, in one line: the only Ethiopian cholera count for a period inside 2026-07-01..11-16
    on ReliefWeb is UNICEF HSR No.4 (Amhara 15/0, Jul-Aug) = existing row 3417. Every regional product repeats frozen
    pre-window figures (WHO dashboard 50/2 last week 2026-03-09..15 -> OCHA ROSEA maps 'Jan-Jul/Sep 2026: 50'; Africa CDC
    15/0 = WHO multi-country update #35 YTD to 2026-02-22). WHO AFRO OEW wk31-41 list no Ethiopia cholera event while Ethiopia
    measles is reported weekly (wk37 article: measles surveillance 'broadly stable') -> cholera silence is non-reporting, not
    absence. Consistent with the main pass and the Agent 7 audit; no zero is justified.
  - UNICEF HAC indicator quirk (for whoever audits row 3417): HSR No.3 (Jan-Jun) narrates 51 Amhara cholera cases treated but
    leaves the HAC 'treated for cholera/AWD' cell blank; HSR No.4 gives Jul-Aug 15 and 'Cumulative to 31 Aug 15', i.e. its
    cumulative restarts in July. Row 3417 (Jul-Aug = 15) is unaffected; do not read 15 as a Jan-Aug total.
  - UNHCR South Sudan Situation #12 (Jul 2026) 'Disease surveillance at border entry points detected no suspected cases' is an
    unnamed-disease Ebola-screening statement - not a cholera zero (same conclusion as main pass B6Q15).
  - Not yet on ReliefWeb at 2026-11-17 (re-check next pass): UNICEF Ethiopia HSR No.5 (Sep-Oct 2026); WHO GHoA Food Insecurity
    and Health Crisis SitRep No.2 (Jul-Sep 2026); UNHCR Ethiopia Jul-Sep 2026 public-health/WASH factsheets; WHO AFRO OEW
    wk42+ (wk27-30 also never posted); Africa CDC EIWR after 24 Sep 2026; OCHA ROSEA snapshot after Oct 2026.
  - Host limitation: reliefweb.int/attachments throttled (HTTP 429) whenever >1 downloader ran - presumably other countries'
    RW top-ups on the same host; gentle single-stream download with backoff completed every item.
